# Assignments

Assignments to a function's arguments, their swizzles and their elements. Godot lets a function assign its arguments. Local variables come with the statements slice (#1824).

TSL nodes hold no state, so the transpiler keeps each argument's current value and writes each assignment there. Godot writes an assignment without brackets into its GLSL (`shader_compiler.cpp:1132`), so GLSL binds the operator beside an assignment to the assignment's value: Godot draws `(a = b) * a` as `a = (b * a)`, and fails to compile `b * (a = b)`. The transpiler reads the text as GLSL does and refuses what GLSL refuses.

Godot 4.6.3-stable (official), probed on llvmpipe (LLVM 20.1.2). Each table shows at most 8 of a probe's rows, picked to show as many mixes of zero, subnormal, normal, infinite and NaN values as fit. The real-GL test (`pnpm test:shaders:gpu`) checks every row, bit for bit, NaN lanes compared as NaN. Generated by `pnpm godot:shader-language`: do not edit by hand.


## `=` (OP_ASSIGN)

`a = b` writes `b` to `a` and gives the new value. A chain `a = b = c` writes `c` to both.

The transpiler emits `the argument value replaced by b`. Rows: `OP_ASSIGN(bool,bool)`, `OP_ASSIGN(bvec2,bvec2)`, `OP_ASSIGN(bvec3,bvec3)`, `OP_ASSIGN(bvec4,bvec4)`, `OP_ASSIGN(int,int)`, `OP_ASSIGN(ivec2,ivec2)`, `OP_ASSIGN(ivec3,ivec3)`, `OP_ASSIGN(ivec4,ivec4)`, `OP_ASSIGN(uint,uint)`, `OP_ASSIGN(uvec2,uvec2)`, `OP_ASSIGN(uvec3,uvec3)`, `OP_ASSIGN(uvec4,uvec4)`, `OP_ASSIGN(float,float)`, `OP_ASSIGN(vec2,vec2)`, `OP_ASSIGN(vec3,vec3)`, `OP_ASSIGN(vec4,vec4)`, `OP_ASSIGN(mat2,mat2)`, `OP_ASSIGN(mat3,mat3)`, `OP_ASSIGN(mat4,mat4)`. Godot source: `servers/rendering/shader_language.cpp:1810`.

Lowered rows:

- `OP_ASSIGN(bool,bool)`: WebGL 2 lowered: written to the argument value, WebGPURenderer lowered: written to the argument value.
- `OP_ASSIGN(bvec2,bvec2)`: WebGL 2 lowered: written to the argument value, WebGPURenderer lowered: written to the argument value.
- `OP_ASSIGN(bvec3,bvec3)`: WebGL 2 lowered: written to the argument value, WebGPURenderer lowered: written to the argument value.

### Probe `unbracketed-operator-after`, 32 input rows

```glsl
vec2 probe(vec2 a, vec2 b) { return (a = b) * a; }
```

| `a` | `b` | Godot gives |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `(0.0, -2.75)` | `(1e-7, 1e-40)` | `(0.0, -0.0)` |
| `(1.0, 1e-7)` | `(16777216.0, -inf)` | `(16777216.0, -inf)` |
| `(0.5, 16777216.0)` | `(-3e+38, 0.0)` | `(-1.5e+38, 0.0)` |
| `(0.33333334, -3e+38)` | `(inf, 1.0)` | `(inf, -3e+38)` |
| `(1e-7, 1e-40)` | `(-inf, -1.0)` | `(-inf, -0.0)` |
| `(123456.79, inf)` | `(NaN, 0.5)` | `(NaN, inf)` |
| `(3e+38, NaN)` | `(-0.0, 0.33333334)` | `(-0.0, NaN)` |
| `(-0.0, 0.33333334)` | `(1e-40, -0.0)` | `(-0.0, -0.0)` |

### Probe `unbracketed-chain`, 32 input rows

```glsl
int probe(int a, int b) { a = b = a + b; return a * b; }
```

| `a` | `b` | Godot gives |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0` | `32` | `1024` |
| `1` | `255` | `65536` |
| `-256` | `0` | `65536` |

### Probe `unbracketed-condition`, 8 input rows

```glsl
bvec2 probe(bool c, bool d, bool a) { return bvec2((c = d) ? a : !a, c); }
```

| `c` | `d` | `a` | Godot gives |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `false` | `true` | `false` | `(false, false)` |
| `true` | `false` | `true` | `(false, false)` |
| `true` | `false` | `false` | `(true, true)` |

## Compound assignments

`a += b`, `a -= b`, `a *= b`, `a /= b`, `a %= b`, `a <<= b`, `a >>= b`, `a &= b`, `a |= b` and `a ^= b` apply the operator to `a` and `b` and write the result to `a`. `v *= m` multiplies a vector by a matrix on the right.

The transpiler emits `the argument value replaced by a.add(b)`. ### Probe `assign-swizzle-compound`, 32 input rows

```glsl
ivec3 probe(ivec3 a, int b) { a.zx *= b; return a; }
```

| `a` | `b` | Godot gives |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `(0, 7, 65536)` | `32` | `(0, 7, 2097152)` |
| `(1, 31, 1000003)` | `255` | `(255, 31, 255000765)` |
| `(31, 1000003, 0)` | `2147483647` | `(2147483617, 1000003, 0)` |
| `(-256, -2147483648, 2)` | `0` | `(0, -2147483648, 0)` |
| `(1000003, 0, 7)` | `-1` | `(-1000003, 0, -7)` |

| `(65535u, 4294967294u)` | `(0u, 31u)` | `(131070u, 4294967263u)` |

| `(0u, 31u)` | `(1000003u, 0u)` | `(1000003u, 62u)` |
